{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1792800641296,"sessionID":"ses_e94c326edffewCQe8aLV20uG3Q","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"write","callID":"call_00_cwdxbt4lva4cxacfvkpzi53v","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/cholesky_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_cuda_r5/cholesky/cholesky.cpp","content":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// GPU Cholesky decomposition (blocked right-looking algorithm).\n// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular.\n//\n// The matrix is stored in row-major order.  The decomposition is split into\n// panels of CH_BLOCK columns.  For every panel three steps are executed on the\n// GPU:\n//   1. factorization of the diagonal block,\n//   2. triangular solve for the panel below the diagonal block,\n//   3. rank-CH_BLOCK update of the trailing sub-matrix.\n// Steps 1 and 2 expose parallelism over rows while step 3 is a tiled\n// triangular GEMM which contains the bulk of the floating point work.\n\nnamespace {\n\nconstexpr int CH_BLOCK = 64;    // panel width\nconstexpr int PANEL_ROWS = 64;  // rows handled by one panel-solve block\nconstexpr int TR_TILE = 32;     // trailing-update tile size\n\n// Zero the strictly upper triangular part of the matrix.\n__global__ void zeroUpperKernel(double* A, int n) {\n    const int i = blockIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n) {\n        return;\n    }\n    for (int j = i + 1 + threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {\n        A[(size_t)i * n + j] = 0.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Factor the b x b diagonal block that starts at column s.\n__global__ void factorDiagonalKernel(double* A, int n, int s, int b, int* flag) {\n    __shared__ double S[CH_BLOCK][CH_BLOCK + 1];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n\n    // Load the lower triangle of the block into shared memory.\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < b * b; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = idx / b;\n        const int c = idx % b;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            S[r][c] = A[(size_t)(s + r) * n + (s + c)];\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int j = 0; j < b; ++j) {\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (int k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n                const double v = S[j][k];\n                sum += v * v;\n            }\n            const double val = S[j][j] - sum;\n            double diag;\n            if (val <= 0.0) {\n                atomicMin(flag, s + j);\n                diag = 1.0;\n            } else {\n                diag = sqrt(val);\n            }\n            S[j][j] = diag;\n            A[(size_t)(s + j) * n + (s + j)] = diag;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        for (int i = j + 1 + tid; i < b; i += blockDim.x) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (int k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n                sum += S[i][k] * S[j][k];\n            }\n            const double v = (S[i][j] - sum) / S[j][j];\n            S[i][j] = v;\n            A[(size_t)(s + i) * n + (s + j)] = v;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n}\n\n// Solve the panel rows below the diagonal block: rows [s + b, n), columns [s, s + b).\n__global__ void panelSolveKernel(double* A, int n, int s, int b) {\n    __shared__ double R[PANEL_ROWS][CH_BLOCK + 1];\n    __shared__ double jrow[CH_BLOCK + 1];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int row = s + b + blockIdx.x * PANEL_ROWS + tid;\n    const bool valid = row < n;\n\n    if (valid) {\n        for (int k = 0; k < b; ++k) {\n            R[tid][k] = A[(size_t)row * n + s + k];\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int j = 0; j < b; ++j) {\n        if (tid < j) {\n            jrow[tid] = A[(size_t)(s + j) * n + s + tid];\n        } else if (tid == j) {\n            jrow[j] = A[(size_t)(s + j) * n + s + j];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (valid) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (int k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n                sum += R[tid][k] * jrow[k];\n            }\n            R[tid][j] = (R[tid][j] - sum) / jrow[j];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (valid) {\n        for (int k = 0; k < b; ++k) {\n            A[(size_t)row * n + s + k] = R[tid][k];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Rank-b update of the trailing lower-triangular sub-matrix, starting at t0.\n__global__ void trailingUpdateKernel(double* A, int n, int s, int b, int t0) {\n    const int ti = blockIdx.x;\n    const int tj = blockIdx.y;\n    if (tj > ti) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const int i0 = t0 + ti * TR_TILE;\n    const int j0 = t0 + tj * TR_TILE;\n\n    __shared__ double Li[TR_TILE][CH_BLOCK + 1];\n    __shared__ double Lj[TR_TILE][CH_BLOCK + 1];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int tid = ty * TR_TILE + tx;\n\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < TR_TILE * b; idx += TR_TILE * TR_TILE) {\n        const int rr = idx / b;\n        const int kk = idx % b;\n        const int gi = i0 + rr;\n        const int gj = j0 + rr;\n        const int gk = s + kk;\n        Li[rr][kk] = (gi < n) ? A[(size_t)gi * n + gk] : 0.0;\n        Lj[rr][kk] = (gj < n) ? A[(size_t)gj * n + gk] : 0.0;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    const int gi = i0 + ty;\n    const int gj = j0 + tx;\n    if (gi < n && gj < n && gi >= gj) {\n        double acc = 0.0;\n        for (int k = 0; k < b; ++k) {\n            acc += Li[ty][k] * Lj[tx][k];\n        }\n        A[(size_t)gi * n + gj] -= acc;\n    }\n}\n\n}  // namespace\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    if (n == 0) {\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    const int ni = static_cast<int>(n);\n    const size_t bytes = n * n * sizeof(double);\n\n    double* dA = nullptr;\n    int* dflag = nullptr;\n    cudaError_t err = cudaMalloc(&dA, bytes);\n    if (err == cudaSuccess) {\n        err = cudaMalloc(&dflag, sizeof(int));\n    }\n    if (err != cudaSuccess) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error: %s\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err));\n        if (dA) cudaFree(dA);\n        if (dflag) cudaFree(dflag);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // flag is -1 while the matrix remains positive definite.\n    cudaMemset(dflag, 0xFF, sizeof(int));\n    err = cudaMemcpy(dA, A.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice);\n    if (err != cudaSuccess) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error: %s\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err));\n        cudaFree(dA);\n        cudaFree(dflag);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    zeroUpperKernel<<<ni, 256>>>(dA, ni);\n\n    for (int s = 0; s < ni; s += CH_BLOCK) {\n        const int b = std::min(CH_BLOCK, ni - s);\n        factorDiagonalKernel<<<1, 256>>>(dA, ni, s, b, dflag);\n\n        const int t0 = s + b;\n        if (t0 < ni) {\n            const int panelRows = ni - t0;\n            const int pblocks = (panelRows + PANEL_ROWS - 1) / PANEL_ROWS;\n            panelSolveKernel<<<pblocks, PANEL_ROWS>>>(dA, ni, s, b);\n\n            const int tiles = (panelRows + TR_TILE - 1) / TR_TILE;\n            const dim3 grid(tiles, tiles);\n            const dim3 block(TR_TILE, TR_TILE);\n            trailingUpdateKernel<<<grid, block>>>(dA, ni, s, b, t0);\n        }\n    }\n\n    err = cudaGetLastError();\n    if (err != cudaSuccess) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error: %s\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err));\n        cudaFree(dA);\n        cudaFree(dflag);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    int hflag = -1;\n    cudaMemcpy(&hflag, dflag, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost);\n    cudaMemcpy(A.data(), dA, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost);\n    cudaFree(dA);\n    cudaFree(dflag);\n\n    if (hflag >= 0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\\n\", hflag);\n        return false;\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n\n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n\n    // Generate random matrix B\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n\n    // Compute A = B * B^T\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i * n + i] += n;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n\n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n\n    // Compute L * L^T\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];\n            }\n            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Compare with original\n    double maxError = 0.0;\n    double relError = 0.0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n\n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n\n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t n = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", n, n);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Allocate matrix\n    std::vector<double> A(n * n);\n    std::vector<double> A_orig;\n\n    // Generate positive definite matrix\n    printf(\"Generating positive definite matrix...\\n\");\n    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);\n\n    if (validate) {\n        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation\n    }\n\n    // Perform Cholesky decomposition\n    printf(\"Computing Cholesky decomposition...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration<double>(end - start);\n\n    if (!success) {\n        printf(\"Cholesky decomposition failed\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %lld ms\\n\", (long long)(duration.count() * 1000.0));\n\n    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)\n    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;\n    double gflops = ops / duration.count() / 1e9;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(A, \"CholeskyL\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n"},"output":"Wrote file successfully.","metadata":{"diagnostics":{},"filepath":"/home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/cholesky_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_cuda_r5/cholesky/cholesky.cpp","exists":true,"truncated":false},"title":"home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/cholesky_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_cuda_r5/cholesky/cholesky.cpp","time":{"start":1792800641167,"end":1792800641265}},"id":"prt_16b3ec51d0012c1GOhNrJzTXuP","sessionID":"ses_e94c326edffewCQe8aLV20uG3Q","messageID":"msg_16b3e35d40014geh01mPqjADtJ"}}

Keeping the block in shared memory avoids the long dependent dot\n// products of the unblocked formulation.\n__global__ void factorDiagonalKernel(double* A, int n, int s, int b, int* flag) {\n    __shared__ double S[CH_BLOCK][CH_BLOCK + 1];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n\n    // Load the lower triangle of the block into shared memory.\n    for (int idx = tid; The decomposition is split into\n// panels of CH_BLOCK columns.  For every panel three steps are executed on the\n// GPU:\n//   1. factorization of the diagonal block,\n//   2. triangular solve for the panel below the diagonal block,\n//   3. rank-CH_BLOCK update of the trailing sub-matrix.\n// Steps 1 and 2 expose parallelism over rows while step 3 is a tiled\n// triangular GEMM which contains the bulk of the floating point work.\n\nnamespace {\n\nconstexpr int CH_BLOCK = 32;    // panel width\nconstexpr int PANEL_ROWS = 64;  // rows handled by one panel-solve block\nconstexpr int TR_TILE = 32;     // trailing-update tile size\n\n// Zero the strictly upper triangular part of the matrix.\n__global__ void zeroUpperKernel(double* A, int n) {\n    const int i = blockIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n) {\n        return;\n    }\n    for (int j = i + 1 + threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {\n        A[(size_t)i * n + j] = 0.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Factor the b x b diagonal block that starts at column s.\n// The block is kept in shared memory and factorized right-looking: after a\n// column is completed, the remaining lower sub-block is updated with an outer\n// product.  Keeping the block in shared memory avoids the long dependent dot\n// products of the unblocked formulation.\n__global__ void factorDiagonalKernel(double* A, int n, int s, int b, int* flag) {\n    __shared__ double S[CH_BLOCK][CH_BLOCK + 1];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n\n    // Load the lower triangle of the block into shared memory.\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < b * b; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = idx / b;\n        const int c = idx % b;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            S[r][c] = A[(size_t)(s + r) * n + (s + c)];\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int j = 0; j < b; ++j) {\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            const double val = S[j][j];\n            double diag;\n            if (val <= 0.0) {\n                atomicMin(flag, s + j);\n                diag = 1.0;\n            } else {\n                diag = sqrt(val);\n            }\n            S[j][j] = diag;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const double inv = 1.0 / S[j][j];\n        for (int i = j + 1 + tid; i < b; i += blockDim.x) {\n            S[i][j] *= inv;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        for (int i = j + 1 + tid; i < b; i += blockDim.x) {\n            const double f = S[i][j];\n            for (int l = j + 1; l <= i; ++l) {\n                S[i][l] -= f * S[l][j];\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < b * b; idx += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = idx / b;\n        const int c = idx % b;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            A[(size_t)(s + r) * n + (s + c)] = S[r][c];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Solve the panel rows below the diagonal block: rows [s + b, n), columns [s, s + b).\n// Every row is independent, so one thread handles one row and no block-wide\n// synchronization is required.\n__global__ void panelSolveKernel(double* A, int n, int s, int b) {\n    __shared__ double R[PANEL_ROWS][CH_BLOCK + 1];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int row = s + b + blockIdx.x * PANEL_ROWS + tid;\n\n    if (row >= n) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    for (int k = 0; k < b; ++k) {\n        R[tid][k] = A[(size_t)row * n + s + k];\n    }\n\n    for (int j = 0; j < b; ++j) {\n        double sum = 0.0;\n        for (int k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n            sum += R[tid][k] * A[(size_t)(s + j) * n + s + k];\n        }\n        R[tid][j] = (R[tid][j] - sum) / A[(size_t)(s + j) * n + s + j];\n    }\n\n    for (int k = 0; k < b; ++k) {\n        A[(size_t)row * n + s + k] = R[tid][k];\n    }\n}\n\n// Rank-b update of the trailing lower-triangular sub-matrix, starting at t0.\n// blockIdx.x enumerates the lower-triangular tile pairs, so no idle upper\n// blocks are launched.\n__global__ void trailingUpdateKernel(double* A, int n, int s, int b, int t0) {\n    const int p = blockIdx.x;\n    int ti = static_cast<int>((sqrt(8.0 * p + 1.0) - 1.0) * 0.5);\n    while (ti * (ti + 1) / 2 > p) {\n        --ti;\n    }\n    while ((ti + 1) * (ti + 2) / 2 <= p) {\n        ++ti;\n    }\n    const int tj = p - ti * (ti + 1) / 2;\n\n    const int i0 = t0 + ti * TR_TILE;\n    const int j0 = t0 + tj * TR_TILE;\n\n    __shared__ double Li[TR_TILE][CH_BLOCK + 1];\n    __shared__ double Lj[TR_TILE][CH_BLOCK + 1];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int tid = ty * TR_TILE + tx;\n\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < TR_TILE * b; idx += TR_TILE * TR_TILE) {\n        const int rr = idx / b;\n        const int kk = idx % b;\n        const int gi = i0 + rr;\n        const int gj = j0 + rr;\n        const int gk = s + kk;\n        Li[rr][kk] = (gi < n) ? A[(size_t)gi * n + gk] : 0.0;\n        Lj[rr][kk] = (gj < n) ? A[(size_t)gj * n + gk] : 0.0;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    const int gi = i0 + ty;\n    const int gj = j0 + tx;\n    if (gi < n && gj < n && gi >= gj) {\n        double acc = 0.0;\n        for (int k = 0;
